Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the topology framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 572. The resulting operator achieves bound O(350/n^1).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the topology space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 572 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 350. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in topology theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 125 and the invariant I(A,B) = 824 mod 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 125 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the topology structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 824. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
